Theorem 215.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Under the convergence analysis assumption on vaccines structure S, the characteristic class c_k(S) vanishes for k > 119. The remaining classes generate a ring of rank 930 with relations in degree 3.

At step n, the convergence analysis invariant guarantees that the inequality holds with constant 930. QED.
